Need projection function exact. Implement water-filling:
```
def project_values(h, target):
    h=np.array(h,float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # If sum close return
    # Use binary search for tau
    lo=np.min(h)-1.0; hi=np.max(h)+1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1))
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h-mid,0,1)
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       mask=(h>1e-15)&(h<1-1e-15)
       cnt=np.count_nonzero(mask)
       if cnt>0:
           h[mask]+=diff/cnt
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
           # if still diff due clip, adjust again
           diff=target-np.sum(h)
           mask=(h>1e-15)&(h<1-1e-15)
           cnt=... if cnt: h[mask]+=diff/cnt
       else:
           # distribute over all maybe
           h += diff/len(h)
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
Binary search on h-mid: As mid increases, sum decreases. Want target. Good. But if target integer and h 0/1, mid may produce interior. Use 60 iters enough. Ensure target within [0,len]. It is.

Possible sum after clip still off by 1e-9. We can final adjust active or if not, use iterative:

`compute_grad_from_weights(h,p0,pp,pn)`:
```
n=len(h); dx=2/n; g=np.zeros(n); g += p0*(1-2h)
# loop over l where weight significant to skip zeros
for l in range(1,n):
   wp=pp[l]; wneg=pn[l]
   if wp==0 and wneg==0: continue
   rem=n-l
   if wp:
      g[:rem] += wp*dx*(1-h[l:l+rem])
      g[l:] -= wp*dx*h[:rem]
   if wneg:
      g[l:] += wneg*dx*(1-h[:rem])
      g[:rem] -= wneg*dx*h[l:l+rem]
return g
```
For symmetric, pos/neg equal; loop all n. Could skip if weights <1e-15. For low temp, few active.

`gradient_polish` top-level:
```
def gradient_polish(g, start_time, budget_s, reserve=15):
    if _use_sym is False: target=_var_target; raw=lambda? no closures. We'll implement generic for current mode.
    best_g=project_values(g,_var_target)
    best_c=raw_c5_current(best_g)
    step=0.1
    no_improve=0
    while time_left(start_time,budget_s,reserve)>5:
       for temp in [0.05,0.01,0.003]: # maybe one pass
           _temp=temp
           x=best_g
           h, f, p0, pp, pn=get_weights(x)
           grad=compute_grad_from_weights(h,p0,pp,pn)
           grad=grad_to_vars(grad)
           grad -= np.mean(grad) # equality direction
           nrm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
           if nrm<1e-10: break
           direction = -grad/nrm
           accepted=False
           for a in (step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125):
              trial=project_values(x + a*direction, _var_target)
              c=raw_c5_current(trial)
              if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                  best_g=trial; best_c=c; accepted=True; break
          if accepted:
              no_improve=0
              step=min(0.2, step*1.1)
          else:
              step*=0.5
              no_improve+=1
              if no_improve>6: break
       if no_improve>6: break
    return best_g,best_c
```
But `get_weights` uses global `_temp`; before computing, set. Good. `raw_c5_current(x)` builds full if sym and raw. Top-level:
```
def raw_c5_current(x):
    h=h_from_vars(x)
    return raw_c5_full(h)
```

We'll call only after final candidate best per n, not every temp. Or after each SLSQP a few steps. Detailed code skeleton:
```

from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_n = 0
_m = 0
_use_sym = False

Functions:
```
def time_left(start,budget,reserve=10.0):
    return max(0.0, float(budget) - (time.time()-float(start)) - float(reserve))
```

`project_values(h,target)`: as above. Need ensure target scalar. If h empty return.
```
def project_values(h, target):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size==0: return h
    target=float(target)
    target=np.clip(target,0.0,h.size)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # quick
    if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
    lo = np.min(h) - 1.0
    hi = np.max(h) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if s > target:
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       mask=(h>1e-14)&(h<1.0-1e-14)
       cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
       if cnt>0:
           h[mask]+=diff/cnt
           h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
       else:
           # eliminate largest fractional? Since no interior, adjust zeros/ones gradually
           if diff>0:
              idx=int(np.argmin(h))
           else:
              idx=int(np.argmax(h))
           # This is crude; but water-filling should avoid no interior unless target at extremes
           h[idx]+=diff if h[idx]+diff>=0 and h[idx]+diff<=1 else np.sign(diff)*1e-12
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final tiny adjustment
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       mask=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
       cnt=...
       if cnt>0: h[mask]+=diff/cnt; h=np.clip
       else:
          # distribute to all?
          h=target? can't.
          # adjust one with slack
          if diff>0:
             idx=int(np.argmin(h))
             add=min(diff,1-h[idx])
             h[idx]+=add
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(h))
             add=max(diff,h[idx]-1)
             h[idx]+=add
    return h
```
Water-filling with binary search should get exact within 1e-12. The no-interior branch likely unnecessary. For target half-integer with binary values, h/mid produce many interior? If h all 0/1, h-mid in [0,1] for ones if mid between0,1; h=0 remain0, h=1 become1-mid interior; count ones >0, so interior. If all zeros and target>0, mid negative could make zeros interior? h=0-mid if mid<0 yields positive; but initial lo=-1, hi=1. Good.

`h_from_vars(x)`:

def h_from_vars(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if _use_sym:
        return np.concatenate((x, x[::-1]))
    return x
```

`grad_to_vars(grad_h)`:
```
def grad_to_vars(grad_h):
    grad_h=np.asarray(grad_h,dtype=float)
    if not _use_sym:
       return grad_h
    m=len(grad_h)//2
    out=grad_h[:m].copy()
    n=len(grad_h)
    for i in range(m):
       out[i] += grad_h[n-1-i]
    return out
```

`raw_c5_full(h)`:
```
def raw_c5_full(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
For n=0 return inf? Not.

def raw_c5_current(x):

`corr_weights_only(h,temp)`:
```
def corr_weights_only(h, temp):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    one_minus=1.0-h
    vp=np.empty(n); vn=np.empty(n)
    vp[0]=dx*np.dot(h, one_minus)
    for l in range(1,n):
       rem=n-l
       vp[l]=dx*np.dot(h[:rem], one_minus[l:l+rem])
       vn[l]=dx*np.dot(h[l:l+rem], one_minus[:rem])
    # mval
    mval=float(vp[0])
    if n>1:
       if vp[1:].max() > mval: mval=float(vp[1:].max())
       if vn[1:].max() > mval: mval=float(vn[1:].max())
    t=1e-12 if temp<=0 else float(temp)
    # To avoid underflow, also if mval large? values bounded
    exps_pos=np.empty(n); exps_neg=np.empty(n)
    exps_pos[0]=np.exp((vp[0]-mval)/t)
    if n>1:
       exps_pos[1:]=np.exp((vp[1:]-mval)/t)
       exps_neg[1:]=np.exp((vn[1:]-mval)/t)
    total=float(exps_pos[0])
    if n>1:
       total += float(np.sum(exps_pos[1:])) + float(np.sum(exps_neg[1:]))
    f = mval + t*np.log(total)
    p0=exps_pos[0]/total
    if n>1:
       pp=exps_pos/total
       pn=exps_neg/total
    else:
       pp=exps_pos; pn=exps_neg
    return f,p0,pp,pn
```
`np.empty` for exps_neg[0] undefined; only pn[0] not used. But pn array length n with uninitialized pn[0], compute_grad skips l=0 for pn. Fine. But returning pn with pn[0] nan? np.empty uninitialized. Set pn[0]=0. Do `exps_neg=np.zeros(n); exps_neg[1:]=...`. `exps_pos=np.zeros(n)`. Use zeros.

If temp very small, exp of differences maybe 1 for active, 0 for others; total at least1, okay.

`get_weights_for_vars(x)`:
```
def get_weights_for_vars(x):
    global _cache_valid, _cache_x, _cache_h, _cache_f, _cache_p0, _cache_pp, _cache_pn, _cache_temp, _cache_nvar
    x_arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if _cache_valid and _cache_temp == _temp and _cache_nvar == x_arr.size and _cache_x is not None:
        # Need also mode same? nvar alone not enough for sym vs full? If sym m and full n could same size? Add _use_sym.
        if _cache_sym == _use_sym and np.array_equal(x_arr, _cache_x):
             return _cache_h, _cache_f, _cache_p0, _cache_pp, _cache_pn
    h=h_from_vars(x_arr)
    f,p0,pp,pn=corr_weights_only(h,_temp)
    _cache_valid=True; _cache_x=x_arr.copy(); _cache_h=h; _cache_f=f; _cache_p0=p0; _cache_pp=pp; _cache_pn=pn; _cache_temp=float(_temp); _cache_nvar=x_arr.size; _cache_sym=bool(_use_sym)
    return h,f,p0,pp,pn
```
Need declare `_cache_sym` global. Add.

`compute_grad_from_weights(h,p0,pp,pn)`: as above.
Potential memory: pp,pn length n. For l where no weight, skip. dx.
```
g = p0*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
for l in range(1,n):
    rem=n-l
    wp=pp[l]; wneg=pn[l]
    if wp == 0.0 and wneg == 0.0: continue
    if wp != 0.0:
        g[:rem] += wp*dx*(1.0-h[l:l+rem])
        g[l:] -= wp*dx*h[:rem]
    if wneg != 0.0:
        g[l:] += wneg*dx*(1.0-h[:rem])
        g[:rem] -= wneg*dx*h[l:l+rem]
return g
```
Check derivative positions for pos: term for i=0..rem-1: h_i*(1-h_{i+l}). d/dh_i =1-h_{i+l}; d/dh_{i+l}=-h_i. So g[0:rem]+=...; g[l:] (positions l..n-1) -= h[0:rem]. correct.
neg: terms h_{i+l}*(1-h_i): d/dh_{i+l}=1-h_i => g[l:] += (1-h[:rem]); d/dh_i=-h_{i+l} => g[:rem] -= h[l:l+rem]. correct.
For l where rem maybe 0? range(1,n) rem>=1.

`interp_to_n`:
```
def interp_to_n(h_values,n):
    old=len(h_values)
    if old == n: return np.array(h_values,float)
    if old == 0: return np.ones(n)*0.5
    x_old=(np.arange(old)+0.5)*2.0/old
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_values)
```

`gradient_polish` uses current mode and _temp set. Need ensure no lambda. Implementation with loops.

Let's write `gradient_polish` carefully:
```
def gradient_polish(g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=20, reserve=15.0):
    g=project_values(g,_var_target)
    best_g=g.copy()
    best_c=raw_c5_current(best_g)
    step=0.08
    no_improve=0
    templist=(0.05,0.015,0.004)
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time_left(start_time,budget_s,reserve) <= 2.0: break
        for temp in templist:
            if step < 1e-5 or no_improve > 8: break
            _temp=temp
            h,f,p0,pp,pn=get_weights_for_vars(best_g)
            grad_h=compute_grad_from_weights(h,p0,pp,pn)
            grad=grad_to_vars(grad_h)
            grad=grad - np.mean(grad)
            nrm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-10:
                step=step*0.5
                continue
            direction = -grad / nrm
            accepted=False
            for a in (step, 0.5*step, 0.25*step, 0.125*step, 0.0625*step):
                trial=project_values(best_g + a*direction, _var_target)
                ctrial=raw_c5_current(trial)
                if ctrial < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_g=trial; best_c=ctrial; accepted=True; break
            if accepted:
                no_improve=0
                if step < 0.15: step = min(0.2, step*1.25)
            else:
                step *= 0.5
                no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 8: break
    return best_g, best_c
```
This may call many raw evaluations; max_iter20*temps3*steps5=300, maybe okay but if many calls heavy. We can reduce templist to (0.03,0.006) and max_iter 10. Since SLSQP already polished. Use `max_iter` parameter small. For final n polish use max_iter=30.

Maybe gradient of smoothed logsumexp at t large not helpful; use temp list based on remaining. Keep.

`raw_c5_current` uses `np.correlate` each time, cost high in polish. For gradient polish, could use our `raw_value_from_weights`? We didn't compute max counts? We can use `raw_c5_from_vals` but need values. Maybe use `np.correlate` in C, okay. For n=192, correlate direct C maybe fast ~0.1ms. 300 calls 30ms. fine.

Potential problem: `raw_c5_current` for sym x builds full h; for polish many times. okay.

Need no lambdas in sort. We'll implement manual:
```
def sort_candidates_by_c(cand_list):
    # cand_list tuples (c,g)
    if len<=1 return
    n=len
    cvals=np.empty(n)
    for i in range(n): cvals[i]=cand_list[i][0]
    order=np.argsort(cvals)
    out=[]
    for i in order: out.append(cand_list[i])
    return out
```

Let's write `optimize_at_n` in detail:
```
def optimize_at_n(n, rng, parent_h, parent_n, best_h, best_c5, best_n, start_time, budget_s):
    global _temp
    set_sym_mode(n)
    m=_m; target=_var_target
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':con_eq_vars,'jac':con_jac_vars}]
    cands_raw=generate_sym_candidates(...)
    cand_list=[]
    for g in cands_raw:
       g=project_values(g,target)
       c=raw_c5_current(g)
       cand_list.append((c,g))
    cand_list=sort_candidates(cand_list)
    limit = 8 if len(cand_list)>8 else len(cand_list)
    local_g = cand_list[0][1].copy() if cand_list else None
    local_c = cand_list[0][0] if cand_list else inf
    for ci in range(limit):
       c0,g = cand_list[ci]
       if c0 < best_c5:
           full=h_from_vars(g).copy()
           best_c5=c0; best_h=full; best_n=n
       g=project_values(g,target)
       # skip SLSQP if raw already best? still polish maybe
       for temp in (0.12,0.04,0.008,0.002):
           if time_left(start_time,budget_s,70) < 35: break
           _temp=float(temp)
           try:
              res=minimize(obj_vars, g, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':140,'ftol':1e-11,'iprint':0})
              g_new=project_values(res.x,target)
           except Exception:
              g_new=project_values(g,target)
           g=g_new
           c=raw_c5_current(g)
           if c < local_c:
               local_c=c; local_g=g.copy()
           if c < best_c5:
               best_c5=c; best_n=n; best_h=h_from_vars(g).copy()
           if temp <= 0.008 and time_left(start_time,budget_s,40) > 20:
              g2,c2=gradient_polish(g,start_time,budget_s,max_iter=12,reserve=30)
              if c2 < c:
                  g=g2; c=c2
                  if c<local_c: ...
                  if c<best: ...
       # If raw c0 near best and no time, continue
    # final best at this n
    if local_g is not None and time_left(start_time,budget_s,120) > 80:
       # one more SLSQP at low temp from local_g
       _temp=0.004
       try: res=minimize(obj_vars, local_g,... options maxiter=200 ftol 1e-12)
          local_g=project(res.x,target); local_c=raw...
       except: pass
       if local_c < best... update
       local_g2, c2=gradient_polish(local_g,start,budget,max_iter=50,reserve=40)
       update
    return best_h,best_c5,best_n
```

def polish_full_initial(h0,n,start_time,budget_s):
    set_full_mode(n)
    target=_var_target
    h=project_values(h0,target)
    c=raw_c5_current(h)
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n; cons=...
    for temp in (0.05,0.012,0.003):
       if time_left(start,budget,60)<30: break
       _temp=temp
       try res=minimize(obj_vars,h,... maxiter=150)
          h=project(res.x,target)
       except: pass
       c=raw_c5_current(h)
    h,c=gradient_polish(h,start,budget,max_iter=10,reserve=30)
    return h,c
```

def sort_candidates(cand_list):
    n=len(cand_list)
    if n <= 1: return cand_list
    cvals=np.empty(n,float)
    for i in range(n): cvals[i]=float(cand_list[i][0])
    order=np.argsort(cvals)
    out=[]
    for i in order: out.append(cand_list[i])
    return out
```

```
def binary_rounding_candidate(h):
   n=len(h); target=n/2.0
   if abs(target-round(target))>1e-12: return None
   want=int(round(target))
   hr=(h>0.5).astype(float)
   ones=int(np.sum(hr))
   if ones>want:
      idx=np.where(hr==1.0)[0]
      if idx.size:
         vals=h[idx]
         order=np.argsort(vals)[:ones-want]
         hr[idx[order]]=0.0
   elif ones<want:
      idx=np.where(hr==0.0)[0]
      if idx.size:
         vals=h[idx]
         order=np.argsort(-vals)[:want-ones]
         hr[idx[order]]=1.0
   return project_values(hr,target)
```

def binary_local_search(g, rng, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=2000, reserve=30):
    m=len(g); target=_var_target (m/2)
    h_full = build from g; c=raw
    # ensure binary with target ones
    g0=np.zeros(m); idx=rng.choice(m,int(target),replace=False); g0[idx]=1
    best_g=g0.copy(); best_c=raw_sym(g0)
    # current g maybe parent? Start from best binary candidate; ensure target.
    temperature=0.01? Objective C difference.
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time left< reserve: break
       i=rng.randint(m); j=rng.randint(m)
       if i==j: continue
       if g[i]==g[j]: continue
       # swap: g[j]=proj, etc. if binary, values 0/1, want move one 1 to i one 0 to j
       g[i],g[j]=g[j],g[i]
       new_c=raw_c5_current(g)
       if new_c<best_c: best_c=new_c; best_g=g.copy()
       elif (new_c-best_c) < temperature: # accept if close? raw C; use probability exp(-(new-best)/temp)
           if rng.rand() < np.exp(-(new_c-best_c)/ (0.005+temperature*0.99)): pass
       else: swap back
       temperature*=0.999? 
    # maybe greedily try all single-pair improvements until no? m=64, pairs ~2k, eval each too many but could for best: try all flips once.
    return best_g,best_c
```

def make_binary_g(rng,m,target):

def binary_sym_search(n, rng, start, budget_s, init_g=None, max_iter=2000, reserve=40):
   # assumes sym mode set
   m=_m; target=_var_target
   if abs(target-round(target))>1e-9: return None,inf
   best_g=None; best_c=inf
   starts=[]
   if init_g is not None:
       g=make_binary_from_parent? maybe round init_g and adjust
   for _ in range(3): starts.append(make_binary_g(...))
   for g0 in starts:
      g=project_values(g0,target) # if binary ok
      c=raw_c5_current(g)
      for it in range(max_iter):
         if time_left<reserve: break
         i=rng.randint(m); j=rng.randint(m)
         if i==j or g[i]==g[j]: continue
         g[i]=1-g[i]; g[j]=1-g[j]
         cnew=raw_c5_current(g)
         if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=cnew; best_g=g.copy()
         if cnew < c - 1e-12:
             c=cnew
         else:
             # accept if cnew - c < 0.0002 and rng.rand()<0.3
             if (cnew - c) < 0.0003 and rng.rand() < 0.5:
                 c=cnew
             else:
                 g[i]=1-g[i]; g[j]=1-g[j]
      # local greedy single swaps until improve? one pass
      for i in range(m):
         if time_left<reserve: break
         vi=g[i]
         for j in range(i+1,m):
            if g[j]==vi: continue
            g[i]=1-vi; g[j]=1-g[j] # wait if vi=1,gj=0 -> i=0,j=1; if vi=0,gj=1 -> i=1,j=0. Since values binary and opposite.
            cnew=raw_c5_current(g)
            if cnew < c - 1e-12:
               c=cnew
               if cnew<best_c: best_c=cnew; best_g=g.copy()
            else:
               g[i]=vi; g[j]=1-vi
    return best_g,best_c
```

`round_to_fixed_count_g` similar to binary rounding but target. It may produce all 0/1 with exact count.

Let's code it. If g has sum close target but fractional, rounding may change count; adjust by switching threshold closest to .5.
```
def round_to_fixed_count(g,target):
    g=np.asarray(g,float)
    if abs(target-round(target))>1e-9: return None
    want=int(round(target))
    gr=(g>0.5).astype(float)
    ones=int(np.sum(gr))
    if ones > want:
       idx=np.where(gr==1.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          order=np.argsort(g[idx])[:ones-want]
          gr[idx[order]]=0.0
    else:
       idx=np.where(gr==0.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          order=np.argsort(-g[idx])[:want-ones]
          gr[idx[order]]=1.0
    # If still count not want due insufficient, adjust using all indices?
    if np.sum(gr)!=want:
       # fallback simple
       gr=np.zeros(len(g)); if want>0: gr[:want]=1.0
    return gr
```
Good.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_n = 0
_m = 0
_use_sym = False
_var_target = 0.0
_temp = 0.05

_cache_valid = False
_cache_x = None
_cache_h = None
_cache_f = 0.0
_cache_p0 = 0.0
_cache_pp = None
_cache_pn = None
_cache_temp = -1.0
_cache_nvar = -1
_cache_sym = False


def time_left(start_time, budget_s, reserve_s=10.0):
    return max(0.0, float(budget_s) - (time.time() - float(start_time)) - float(reserve_s))


def project_values(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    target = float(target)
    target = min(max(target, 0.0), float(h.size))
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - target) <= 1e-12:
        return h

    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    for _ in range(80):
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)

    h = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(50):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt <= 0:
            break
        h[mask] = h[mask] + diff / float(cnt)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] = h[mask] + diff / float(cnt)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def set_sym_mode(n):
    global _n, _m, _use_sym, _var_target, _cache_valid, _cache_sym
    _n = int(n)
    _m = _n // 2
    _use_sym = True
    _var_target = float(_m) / 2.0
    _cache_valid = False
    _cache_sym = True


def set_full_mode(n):
    global _n, _m, _use_sym, _var_target, _cache_valid, _cache_sym
    _n = int(n)
    _m = 0
    _use_sym = False
    _var_target = float(_n) / 2.0
    _cache_valid = False
    _cache_sym = False


def h_from_vars(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if _use_sym:
        return np.concatenate((x, x[::-1]))
    return x


def grad_to_vars(grad_h):
    grad_h = np.asarray(grad_h, dtype=float)
    if not _use_sym:
        return grad_h
    n = len(grad_h)
    m = n // 2
    out = grad_h[:m].copy()
    for i in range(m):
        out[i] += grad_h[n - 1 - i]
    return out


def raw_c5_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def raw_c5_current(x):
    return raw_c5_full(h_from_vars(x))


def con_eq_vars(x):
    return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))) - _var_target


def con_jac_vars(x):
    return np.ones(np.asarray(x).shape, dtype=float)


def corr_weights_only(h, temp):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    one_minus = 1.0 - h

    vp = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    vn = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    vp[0] = dx * np.dot(h, one_minus)
    for l in range(1, n):
        rem = n - l
        vp[l] = dx * np.dot(h[:rem], one_minus[l:l + rem])
        vn[l] = dx * np.dot(h[l:l + rem], one_minus[:rem])

    mval = float(vp[0])
    if n > 1:
        mval = max(mval, float(np.max(vp[1:])), float(np.max(vn[1:])))

    t = float(temp)
    if t <= 1e-14:
        t = 1e-14

    exp_pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    exp_neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    exp_pos[0] = np.exp((vp[0] - mval) / t)
    if n > 1:
        exp_pos[1:] = np.exp((vp[1:] - mval) / t)
        exp_neg[1:] = np.exp((vn[1:] - mval) / t)

    total = float(exp_pos[0])
    if n > 1:
        total += float(np.sum(exp_pos[1:])) + float(np.sum(exp_neg[1:]))
    if total <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(total):
        total = 1.0

    f = float(mval + t * np.log(total))
    p0 = float(exp_pos[0] / total)
    pp = exp_pos / total
    pn = exp_neg / total
    return f, p0, pp, pn


def get_weights_for_vars(x):
    global _cache_valid, _cache_x, _cache_h, _cache_f, _cache_p0, _cache_pp, _cache_pn
    global _cache_temp, _cache_nvar, _cache_sym

    x_arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if (_cache_valid and _cache_sym == _use_sym and _cache_nvar == x_arr.size and
            _cache_temp == _temp and _cache_x is not None and
            np.array_equal(x_arr, _cache_x)):
        return _cache_h, _cache_f, _cache_p0, _cache_pp, _cache_pn

    h = h_from_vars(x_arr)
    f, p0, pp, pn = corr_weights_only(h, _temp)

    _cache_valid = True
    _cache_x = x_arr.copy()
    _cache_h = h
    _cache_f = f
    _cache_p0 = p0
    _cache_pp = pp
    _cache_pn = pn
    _cache_temp = float(_temp)
    _cache_nvar = int(x_arr.size)
    _cache_sym = bool(_use_sym)
    return h, f, p0, pp, pn


def compute_grad_from_weights(h, p0, pp, pn):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    g = float(p0) * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    for l in range(1, n):
        rem = n - l
        wp = float(pp[l])
        wn = float(pn[l])
        if wp <= 1e-16 and wn <= 1e-16:
            continue

        if wp > 1e-16:
            g[:rem] = g[:rem] + wp * dx * (1.0 - h[l:l + rem])
            g[l:] = g[l:] - wp * dx * h[:rem]

        if wn > 1e-16:
            g[l:] = g[l:] + wn * dx * (1.0 - h[:rem])
            g[:rem] = g[:rem] - wn * dx * h[l:l + rem]

    return g


def obj_vars(x):
    _, f, _, _, _ = get_weights_for_vars(x)
    return f


def jac_vars(x):
    h, _, p0, pp, pn = get_weights_for_vars(x)
    gh = compute_grad_from_weights(h, p0, pp, pn)
    return grad_to_vars(gh)


def interp_to_n(h_values, n):
    h_values = np.asarray(h_values, dtype=float)
    old = len(h_values)
    n = int(n)
    if old == n:
        return h_values.copy()
    if old <= 0:
        return 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(old))
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_values)


def symmetrize_values(h_values, n):
    h_values = np.asarray(h_values, dtype=float)
    h_values = h_values[:n]
    return project_values(0.5 * (h_values + h_values[::-1]), float(n) / 2.0)


def make_triangle_full(n):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    return project_values(h, float(n) / 2.0)


def sort_candidates(cand_list):
    n = len(cand_list)
    if n <= 1:
        return cand_list
    cvals = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        cvals[i] = float(cand_list[i][0])
    order = np.argsort(cvals)
    out = []
    for i in order:
        out.append(cand_list[i])
    return out


def round_to_fixed_count(g, target):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    if abs(float(target) - round(float(target))) > 1e-9:
        return None
    want = int(round(float(target)))
    gr = (g > 0.5).astype(float)
    ones = int(np.sum(gr))
    if ones > want:
        idx = np.where(gr == 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            need = ones - want
            order = np.argsort(g[idx])[:need]
            gr[idx[order]] = 0.0
    elif ones < want:
        idx = np.where(gr == 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            need = want - ones
            order = np.argsort(-g[idx])[:need]
            gr[idx[order]] = 1.0
    if int(np.sum(gr)) != want:
        gr = np.zeros(g.shape, dtype=float)
        if want > 0:
            gr[:min(want, len(gr))] = 1.0
    return gr


def make_binary_g(rng, m, target):
    if abs(float(target) - round(float(target))) > 1e-9:
        return None
    want = int(round(float(target)))
    if want <= 0:
        return np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    if want >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    g = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    idx = rng.choice(m, size=want, replace=False)
    g[idx] = 1.0
    return g


def max_iter_for(m):
    if m <= 40:
        return 220
    if m <= 70:
        return 180
    if m <= 100:
        return 140
    return 110


def generate_sym_candidates(n, rng, parent_h, parent_n, best_h, best_n):
    m = int(n) // 2
    target = float(m) / 2.0
    xg = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    cands = []

    if parent_h is not None and parent_n > 0:
        hp = interp_to_n(parent_h, n)
        hp = symmetrize_values(hp, n)
        cands.append(project_values(hp[:m], target))

    if best_h is not None and best_n > 0:
        hb = interp_to_n(best_h, n)
        hb = symmetrize_values(hb, n)
        cands.append(project_values(hb[:m], target))

    cands.append(project_values(xg.copy(), target))
    cands.append(project_values(0.5 * (1.0 - np.cos(np.pi * xg)), target))

    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 2.0, 4.0):
        cands.append(project_values(xg ** p, target))
        cands.append(project_values(1.0 - (1.0 - xg) ** p, target))

    for _trial in range(3):
        g = 0.5 * np.ones(m, dtype=float)
        for j in range(1, 5):
            amp = 0.32 / (0.5 + 0.5 * rng.rand()) * (1.0 / (float(j) * (0.7 + 0.6 * rng.rand())))
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            g = g + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(j) * xg + phase)
        cands.append(project_values(g, target))

    for _trial in range(2):
        g = 0.5 + 0.7 * rng.randn(m)
        k = max(3, m // 20)
        ker = np.ones(k, dtype=float) / float(k)
        g = np.convolve(g, ker, mode="same")
        cands.append(project_values(g, target))

    for _trial in range(2):
        g = make_binary_g(rng, m, target)
        if g is not None:
            cands.append(project_values(g, target))

    return cands


def gradient_polish(g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=20, reserve_s=20.0):
    global _temp
    g = project_values(g, _var_target)
    best_g = g.copy()
    best_c = raw_c5_current(best_g)
    step = 0.08
    no_improve = 0
    temps = (0.03, 0.008, 0.002)
    alphas = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625)

    for _it in range(max_iter):
        if time_left(start_time, budget_s, reserve_s) <= 3.0:
            break
        if step < 1e-6 or no_improve > 8:
            break
        accepted_any = False
        for temp in temps:
            _temp = float(temp)
            h, _, p0, pp, pn = get_weights_for_vars(best_g)
            gh = compute_grad_from_weights(h, p0, pp, pn)
            grad = grad_to_vars(gh)
            grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if nrm < 1e-10:
                step *= 0.5
                no_improve += 2
                break
            direction = -grad / nrm
            accepted = False
            for a in alphas:
                trial = project_values(best_g + (step * a) * direction, _var_target)
                ctry = raw_c5_current(trial)
                if ctry < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_g = trial
                    best_c = ctry
                    accepted = True
                    accepted_any = True
                    break
            if accepted:
                no_improve = 0
                if step < 0.18:
                    step = min(0.22, step * 1.3)
            else:
                step *= 0.5
                no_improve += 1
        if no_improve > 8:
            break
    return best_g, best_c


def binary_sym_search(n, rng, start_time, budget_s, init_g=None, reserve_s=60.0):
    m = int(n) // 2
    target = float(m) / 2.0
    if abs(target - round(target)) > 1e-9:
        return None, float("inf")

    starts = []
    if init_g is not None:
        gr = round_to_fixed_count(init_g, target)
        if gr is not None:
            starts.append(project_values(gr, target))

    for _trial in range(2):
        g = make_binary_g(rng, m, target)
        if g is not None:
            starts.append(project_values(g, target))

    if len(starts) == 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_g = starts[0].copy()
    best_c = raw_c5_current(best_g)
    passes = 1 if m > 60 else 2

    for g0 in starts:
        g = project_values(g0, target)
        c = raw_c5_current(g)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_g = g.copy()

        stop = False
        for _pass in range(passes):
            if stop:
                break
            for i in range(m):
                if time_left(start_time, budget_s, reserve_s) <= 2.0:
                    stop = True
                    break
                for j in range(i + 1, m):
                    vi = float(g[i])
                    vj = float(g[j])
                    if vi == vj:
                        continue
                    old_i = vi
                    old_j = vj
                    g[i] = old_j
                    g[j] = old_i
                    cn = raw_c5_current(g)
                    if cn < c - 1e-12:
                        c = cn
                        if cn < best_c:
                            best_c = cn
                            best_g = g.copy()
                    else:
                        g[i] = old_i
                        g[j] = old_j
            if time_left(start_time, budget_s, reserve_s) <= 15.0:
                break

    return best_g, best_c


def polish_full_initial(h0, n, start_time, budget_s, rng):
    global _temp
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    set_full_mode(n)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    h = project_values(h0, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = raw_c5_current(best_h)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': con_eq_vars, 'jac': con_jac_vars}]

    for temp in (0.05, 0.015, 0.004):
        if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 80) < 35:
            break
        _temp = float(temp)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_vars,
                h,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=jac_vars,
                options={'maxiter': max_iter_for(n), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'iprint': 0}
            )
            h_new = project_values(res.x, target)
        except Exception:
            h_new = project_values(h, target)
        h = h_new
        c = raw_c5_current(h)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()

    h2, c2 = gradient_polish(best_h, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=12, reserve_s=45.0)
    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_h = h2
        best_c = c2

    for _trial in range(2):
        if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 70) < 35:
            break
        noise_scale = 0.02 if _trial == 0 else 0.05
        h_try = project_values(best_h + noise_scale * rng.randn(n), target)
        _temp = 0.006
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_vars,
                h_try,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=jac_vars,
                options={'maxiter': max_iter_for(n), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'iprint': 0}
            )
            h_try = project_values(res.x, target)
        except Exception:
            pass
        c_try = raw_c5_current(h_try)
        if c_try < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h_try
            best_c = c_try

    return best_h, best_c


def optimize_at_n(n, rng, parent_h, parent_n, best_h, best_c5, best_n, start_time, budget_s):
    global _temp

    set_sym_mode(int(n))
    m = _m
    target = _var_target
    use_binary = (n in (64, 96, 128)) and time_left(start_time, budget_s, 320) > 180

    candidates = generate_sym_candidates(n, rng, parent_h, parent_n, best_h, best_n)
    cand_list = []
    for g in candidates:
        g = project_values(g, target)
        c = raw_c5_current(g)
        cand_list.append((c, g))

    if use_binary:
        init_g = None
        if len(cand_list) > 0:
            init_g = cand_list[0][1]
        bg, bc = binary_sym_search(n, rng, start_time, budget_s, init_g=init_g, reserve_s=70.0)
        if bg is not None and np.isfinite(bc):
            bg = project_values(bg, target)
            bc = raw_c5_current(bg)
            cand_list.append((bc, bg))

    cand_list = sort_candidates(cand_list)
    if len(cand_list) == 0:
        return best_h, best_c5, best_n

    limit = 8 if len(cand_list) > 8 else len(cand_list)
    local_g = cand_list[0][1].copy()
    local_c = float(cand_list[0][0])

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': con_eq_vars, 'jac': con_jac_vars}]
    temps = (0.05, 0.015, 0.004, 0.001)

    for ci in range(limit):
        c0, g = cand_list[ci]
        if c0 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = float(c0)
            best_h = h_from_vars(g).copy()
            best_n = int(n)

        g = project_values(g, target)
        for temp in temps:
            if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 80) < 35:
                break
            _temp = float(temp)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    obj_vars,
                    g,
                    method='SLSQP',
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    jac=jac_vars,
                    options={'maxiter': max_iter_for(m), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'iprint': 0}
                )
                g_new = project_values(res.x, target)
            except Exception:
                g_new = project_values(g, target)

            g = g_new
            c = raw_c5_current(g)
            if c < local_c - 1e-12:
                local_c = float(c)
                local_g = g.copy()
            if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = float(c)
                best_h = h_from_vars(g).copy()
                best_n = int(n)

            if temp <= 0.008 and time_left(start_time, budget_s, 45) > 20:
                g2, c2 = gradient_polish(g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=10, reserve_s=35.0)
                if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                    g = g2
                    c = c2
                    if c < local_c - 1e-12:
                        local_c = float(c)
                        local_g = g.copy()
                    if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                        best_c5 = float(c)
                        best_h = h_from_vars(g).copy()
                        best_n = int(n)

    if local_g is not None and time_left(start_time, budget_s, 130) > 70:
        _temp = 0.003
        try:
            res = minimize(
                obj_vars,
                local_g,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=jac_vars,
                options={'maxiter': max_iter_for(m) + 30, 'ftol': 1e-11, 'iprint': 0}
            )
            local_g = project_values(res.x, target)
            local_c = raw_c5_current(local_g)
        except Exception:
            local_g = project_values(local_g, target)
            local_c = raw_c5_current(local_g)

        if local_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = float(local_c)
            best_h = h_from_vars(local_g).copy()
            best_n = int(n)

        local_g, local_c = gradient_polish(local_g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=45, reserve_s=55.0)
        if local_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = float(local_c)
            best_h = h_from_vars(local_g).copy()
            best_n = int(n)

        for _trial in range(2):
            if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 70) < 35:
                break
            noise_scale = 0.015 if _trial == 0 else 0.04
            g_try = project_values(local_g + noise_scale * rng.randn(m), target)
            _temp = 0.005
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    obj_vars,
                    g_try,
                    method='SLSQP',
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    jac=jac_vars,
                    options={'maxiter': max_iter_for(m), 'ftol': 1e-10, 'iprint': 0}
                )
                g_try = project_values(res.x, target)
            except Exception:
                pass
            c_try = raw_c5_current(g_try)
            if c_try < local_c - 1e-12:
                local_c = float(c_try)
                local_g = g_try.copy()
            if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = float(c_try)
                best_h = h_from_vars(g_try).copy()
                best_n = int(n)

        local_g, local_c = gradient_polish(local_g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=35, reserve_s=45.0)
        if local_c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = float(local_c)
            best_h = h_from_vars(local_g).copy()
            best_n = int(n)

    return best_h, best_c5, best_n


def binary_rounding_candidate(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    if abs(target - round(target)) > 1e-9:
        return None
    want = int(round(target))
    hr = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    ones = int(np.sum(hr))
    if ones > want:
        idx = np.where(hr == 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(h[idx])[:ones - want]
            hr[idx[order]] = 0.0
    elif ones < want:
        idx = np.where(hr == 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(-h[idx])[:want - ones]
            hr[idx[order]] = 1.0
    return project_values(hr, target)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    parent_h = None
    parent_n = 0
    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        parent_n = len(parent_h)
        if parent_n < 0:
            parent_h = None
            parent_n = 0
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None
        parent_n = 0

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    if parent_h is not None and parent_n > 0:
        hp = project_values(parent_h, float(parent_n) / 2.0)
        cp = raw_c5_full(hp)
        if cp < best_c5:
            best_h = hp.copy()
            best_c5 = float(cp)
            best_n = int(parent_n)

        hsym = symmetrize_values(hp, parent_n)
        cs = raw_c5_full(hsym)
        if cs < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = hsym.copy()
            best_c5 = float(cs)
            best_n = int(parent_n)

        if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 300) > 90:
            hp2, cp2 = polish_full_initial(hp, parent_n, start_time, budget_s, rng)
            if cp2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h = hp2.copy()
                best_c5 = float(cp2)
                best_n = int(parent_n)
    else:
        n0 = 96
        h0 = make_triangle_full(n0)
        c0 = raw_c5_full(h0)
        best_h = h0.copy()
        best_c5 = float(c0)
        best_n = n0

    main_ns = [96, 128]
    if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 300) > 220:
        main_ns.append(160)
    if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 200) > 80:
        main_ns.append(64)
    if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 300) > 230:
        main_ns.append(192)

    for nn in main_ns:
        if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 130) < 80:
            break
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = optimize_at_n(
            nn, rng, parent_h, parent_n,
            best_h, best_c5, best_n,
            start_time, budget_s
        )

    if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 60) > 30:
        if best_n > 0 and (best_n % 2 == 0):
            nfin = int(best_n)
            hfin = project_values(best_h, float(nfin) / 2.0)
            hsym = symmetrize_values(hfin, nfin)
            csym = raw_c5_full(hsym)
            if csym < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h = hsym.copy()
                best_c5 = float(csym)
                best_n = nfin

            if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 60) > 30:
                set_sym_mode(nfin)
                m = nfin // 2
                target = float(m) / 2.0
                g = project_values(best_h[:m], target)
                g, c = gradient_polish(g, start_time, budget_s, max_iter=50, reserve_s=35.0)
                if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h_from_vars(g).copy()
                    best_c5 = float(c)
                    best_n = nfin
        else:
            nfin = int(best_n)
            hfin = project_values(best_h, float(nfin) / 2.0)
            hp2, cp2 = polish_full_initial(
                hfin, nfin, start_time, budget_s, rng
            )
            if cp2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_h = hp2.copy()
                best_c5 = float(cp2)
                best_n = nfin

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = make_triangle_full(96)
        best_n = 96
        best_c5 = raw_c5_full(best_h)

    best_h = project_values(best_h, float(best_n) / 2.0)

    if time_left(start_time, budget_s, 15) > 5:
        try:
            br = binary_rounding_candidate(best_h)
            if br is not None:
                cbr = raw_c5_full(br)
                if cbr < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_h = br.copy()
                    best_c5 = float(cbr)
                    best_n = int(len(br))
        except Exception:
            pass

    final_c5 = raw_c5_full(best_h)
    try:
        ec5 = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        ec5 = float(ec5)
        if np.isfinite(ec5) and ec5 < final_c5:
            final_c5 = ec5
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
